TL;DR: 本文提出了一种质量受控的LLM流水线用于食材数据采集，通过稳健统计估计、领域特定不变性检查和网络回退机制，将单次LLM输出的不可靠性转化为可操作的工程控制。在30种食材的参考集上，营养标志精确匹配率达98.4%，营养比值中位数绝对百分比误差从31.9%降至10.1%，API成本约每食材1美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前营养数据库不完整、不一致且为人类参考而非自动推理设计；LLM虽能填补数据空白但单次输出不可靠，会向下游计算传播静默错误。

Method: 设计了质量受控的LLM流水线，包括：(1) 基于233道食谱的Heap定律拟合揭示独特食材增长呈亚线性；(2) 对每种食材属性进行多次LLM查询，将其视为模型诱导的答案分布样本，对数值、布尔、多选、开放类别和可选整数类型应用稳健点估计和归一化置信度；(3) 不变性守护层强制营养和逻辑自一致性，通过线性规划最小化最坏情况百分比偏差来调和轻微数值不一致，严重违规则升级到网络证据修复，失败后再转人工审核。

Result: 在30种食材的参考集上，管道在营养标志上达到98.4%精确匹配；营养比值的中位数绝对百分比误差从基线（中位数聚合）的31.9%降至10.1%，降低了21.8个百分点；API成本约为每食材1美元。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的数据库构建可以被视为一个受控的数据工程工作流，使不确定性变得可操作而非丢弃，为计算营养学提供了可靠的数据采集框架。

Abstract: Computational nutrition needs precise ingredient data, but current databases are incomplete, inconsistent, and built for human reference rather than automated reasoning. LLMs could help fill these gaps, but single-pass outputs are unreliable and can introduce silent errors into downstream computation. We present a quality-controlled LLM pipeline for ingredient data acquisition that combines robust statistical estimation, domain-specific invariant checks, and a web-fetch fallback. An illustrative Heap's Law fit to 233 recipes suggests that unique-ingredient growth is sub-linear and front-loaded: the projected ratio of unique ingredients to recipes falls from 1.74 at 100 recipes to 0.19 at 5,000. For each ingredient attribute, repeated LLM queries are treated as samples from a model-induced answer distribution, and we apply robust point estimators and normalised confidence scores across numerical, Boolean, multiple-choice, open categorical, and optional integer types. An invariant guard layer enforces nutritional and logical self-consistency within each ingredient record. Minor numeric inconsistencies are reconciled via a linear program that minimises worst-case percentage deviation while preserving semantic zeros, and major violations are escalated to web-evidence-grounded repair, then human review only if that fails. On a curated 30-ingredient reference set, the pipeline achieves 98.4% exact match on nutrient flags and cuts median absolute percentage error on nutrient ratios from 31.9% for the median-aggregated baseline to 10.1%, a reduction of 21.8 percentage points, at an API cost of about $1 per ingredient. This frames LLM-assisted database construction as a controlled data-engineering workflow that makes uncertainty operational rather than discarding it.

</details>


### [203] [Choosing a Text Embedding Model: A Practical Benchmarking and Decision Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23507)
*Madhav S Baidya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于实证的嵌入模型选择框架，通过对比商业API模型(T3EM)与开源替代方案在检索任务上的表现，并置于MTEB基准测试背景下，系统分析了从嵌入生成、索引搜索到文档分块的全流程，最终给出根据任务、延迟、成本和部署约束选择嵌入模型的实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择文本嵌入模型是构建检索或搜索系统中最重要却最常被忽视的决策之一，排行榜榜首的模型往往并非特定部署场景的最佳选择，因此需要建立实用的、基于证据的模型选择框架。

Method: 基于基准测试研究，评估T3EM（商业API嵌入模型）与多种开源替代方案在英语检索任务上的表现，并将结果置于MTEB（大规模文本嵌入基准）的多种任务（分类、聚类、语义相似度、重排序、对分类、双文本挖掘、摘要）背景下进行分析。同时追踪从嵌入模型到检索结果的完整路径，包括嵌入生成、大规模索引与搜索、文档分块策略。

Result: 形成了根据任务类型、延迟要求、成本和部署约束选择嵌入模型的整合性实用建议集。

Conclusion: 嵌入模型的选择不应仅基于原始基准分数，而应作为完整检索流水线中的一个决策来综合考虑；报告给出了面向实际部署的实用建议。

Abstract: Choosing the right text embedding model is one of the most consequential -- and most frequently under-examined -- decisions in building a retrieval or search system, yet the model that tops a leaderboard is rarely the best choice for a given deployment. This report develops a practical, evidence-based framework for embedding model selection, built on a benchmarking study that evaluates T3EM (Text 3 Embedding Model), a commercial API-based embedding model, against a broad set of open-source alternatives on English-language retrieval tasks, and situates these findings within the wider Massive Text Embedding Benchmark (MTEB) landscape spanning classification, clustering, semantic similarity, reranking, pair classification, bitext mining, and summarization. Beyond raw benchmark scores, the report traces the full path from embedding model to retrieved result -- how embeddings are produced, how they are indexed and searched at scale, and how document chunking strategy shapes retrieval quality -- so that model choice can be reasoned about as one decision within a complete retrieval pipeline rather than in isolation. The result is a consolidated set of practical recommendations for selecting an embedding model according to task, latency, cost, and deployment constraints.

</details>


### [204] [Towards a Relevance Posterior in Neural Information Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23561)
*Andrew Parry,Emmanouil Georgios Lionis,Debasis Ganguly,Sean MacAvaney*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出将现代信息检索系统中的相关性计算重新理解为近似后验推断，通过显式分解查询相关的似然和查询无关的先验，将计算从在线转移到离线，并通过分数融合提升检索和重排序效果（TREC DL-2019/2020上nDCG@10提升最高0.054）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经排序模型计算成本日益高昂，检索管道包含多阶段排序、推荐和检索增强生成等环节，但传统查询时打分（query-time scoring）的单一视角限制了系统设计空间。作者认为现代信息访问系统更适合被理解为近似后验推断，通过显式的似然-先验分解可以重新分配计算资源。

Method: 将经典概率检索形式化框架扩展到现代学习系统，提出显式的似然-先验分解：查询相关的似然（query-dependent likelihood）和查询无关的先验（query-independent prior）。先验通过学习文档的独立效用（query-independent document utility）实现，以分数融合（score fusion）的方式与现有排序器结合，几乎不增加查询时计算开销。

Result: 在TREC DL-2019上，优先先验通过排序融合提升第一阶段检索nDCG@10达0.046；在TREC DL-2020上提升0.029。在下游重排序中，对LLM重排序器RankZephyr的提升最大，TREC DL-2020上nDCG@10提升达0.054。

Conclusion: 显式的似然-先验分解能够将计算从在线转移到离线，同时解耦文档级和交互级信念。该框架与更广泛的信息访问场景相连接，为设计明确分配离线先验与在线交互建模能力的检索系统提供了研究方向。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval systems typically operationalise relevance as a query-conditional score computed at inference time. This design choice has become dominant such that alternative decompositions of relevance are rarely discussed, despite the long history of document and query priors in probabilistic retrieval and large-scale search. As neural ranking models grow more computationally expensive and retrieval pipelines expand to include multi-stage ranking, recommendation, and retrieval-augmented generation, this monolithic view of query-time scoring becomes increasingly limiting. We argue that modern information access systems are more naturally understood as performing approximate posterior inference, in which relevance is refined through a staged combination of query-dependent likelihoods and query-independent priors. We extend classical probabilistic retrieval formalisms to contemporary learned systems and show how explicit likelihood-prior decomposition exposes new opportunities to shift computation offline while disentangling document-level and interaction-level beliefs. We present empirical evidence that incorporating query-independent document utility can complement existing rankers and improve effectiveness with minimal query-time computation (solely score fusion). Concretely, a learned prior improves first-stage retrieval through rank fusion (up to 0.046 nDCG@10 on TREC DL-2019 and 0.029 nDCG@10 on TREC DL-2020) and also improves downstream re-ranking, with the largest gains observed for the LLM re-ranker RankZephyr (up to 0.054 nDCG@10 on TREC DL-2020). Finally, we discuss how this decomposition connects to broader information access and outline research directions for designing retrieval systems that explicitly allocate modelling capacity between offline priors and online interaction.

</details>


### [205] [Melo: A Production LLM-Powered Music Recommendation Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23718)
*Shijia Wang,Da Guo,Qiang Xiao,Fanghui Bi,Weisheng Li,Dongjing Wang,Chuanjiang Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了网易云音乐部署的LLM驱动的音乐推荐智能体Melo，采用确定性五节点状态图架构和提示词/状态机驱动的编排策略。针对实体幻觉和长尾退化两大生产故障模式，提出了推理时实体接地和反射重试两种互补机制。在线A/B测试显示播放列表留存指标提升超2个百分点，核心参与度指标提升超1分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业规模的LLM驱动的音乐推荐系统中，瓶颈不在于模型的智能程度，而在于系统如何检测和从模型错误中恢复。两种关键故障模式——实体幻觉（智能体采纳了不被实时目录或用户行为索引支持的解读）和长尾退化（过度约束的请求退化为通用流行推荐）——驱动了本文的设计。

Method: Melo采用确定性五节点状态图架构，通过提示词和状态机驱动编排策略（而非微调控制器）。核心机制包括：(1) 推理时实体接地——将生产搜索索引作为验证原语，在实体决策向下游传播前进行门控；(2) 反射重试——将断裂工具链中的失败原因言语化并反馈到下一步规划中，从而放松或修改约束而非盲目退回到通用推荐。

Result: 在网易云音乐播放列表场景的为期一月在线A/B测试中，主要播放列表留存指标提升超2个百分点，核心播放列表参与度指标提升超1分钟。离线消融实验显示三层接地堆栈在评估集上使实体误识别降低7.8个百分点；触发会话分析显示反射重试在5.8%的会话中被触发，其中59%实现了流程级恢复。

Conclusion: 在此规模的LLM驱动的音乐推荐中，进展不仅取决于智能体"大脑"本身的能力，同样取决于可命名、可消融的运行时机制，这些机制负责捕获和纠正大脑的错误。这一假设供社区进一步验证。

Abstract: We describe Melo, an LLM-powered music recommendation agent deployed on NetEase Cloud Music. Melo is structured as a deterministic five-node state graph over heterogeneous tools, with a prompt- and state-machine-driven orchestration policy rather than a fine-tuned controller. At industrial scale, the bottleneck is not how smart the brain is but how the system detects and recovers from the mistakes that brain makes. Two production failure modes drove the design: entity hallucination, where the agent commits to interpretations unsupported by the live catalog or user-behavior index, and long-tail degradation, where over-constrained requests collapse to generic popular fallbacks. We address them with two complementary mechanisms. Inference-time entity grounding repurposes the production search index as a verification primitive that gates entity decisions before they propagate downstream. Reflective retry verbalizes failure reasons from a broken tool chain and feeds them into the next planning step, so the system can relax or revise constraints rather than fall back blindly. A one-month online A/B test across NetEase Cloud Music's playlist surfaces reports an over 2 pp lift in a primary playlist retention metric and a lift of over one minute in a core playlist engagement metric. Offline ablation isolates a 7.8 pp reduction in entity misidentification from the three-layer grounding stack on our evaluation set, and a triggered-session analysis on our evaluation set shows reflective retry firing on 5.8% of sessions with 59% process-level recovery. Our deployment experience suggests that progress on LLM-powered music recommendation at this scale depends as much on the named, ablatable runtime machinery that catches and corrects the brain's mistakes as on the brain itself: a hypothesis we offer for the community to test.

</details>


### [206] [Breaking the Loop: An Empirical Comparison of Strategies for Novelty and Freshness in YouTube Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23749)
*Srivaths Ranganathan,Zihuan Diao,Bernardo Cunha,Joshua L. Moore,Robin Dumas,Murat Goksedef,Yanwei Song,Mukai Lu,Gergo Varady,Tracy Pesin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文通过YouTube Music首页的在线A/B测试，系统评估了六种干预措施及四种概念层（服务、训练、架构、探索）的组合实验，发现服务层干预被持续学习循环中和，架构去偏能减少流行度主导但无法创造发现，而基于SNGP的不确定性驱动探索能最大提升新内容曝光但伴随参与度或多样性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续训练的排序模型在音乐推荐系统中会陷入反馈循环，使已消费内容主导推荐，抑制新发布内容（时间新鲜度）和未收听目录项（新奇性）。现有各干预措施虽在学术环境中被充分理解，但在实际系统中面临持续内容摄入、组件互联和实践限制等挑战，缺乏系统性比较研究。

Method: 在YouTube Music首页进行离线策略的在线A/B测试，测试六种独立干预措施及跨四个概念层（服务、训练、架构、探索）的组合实验。所有干预均修改排序模型或消费其分数的服务层，候选生成和其他上游组件保持固定。

Result: 1) 持续训练系统中，服务层干预被学习循环中和；2) 架构去偏减少流行度主导并提升多样性，但不创造内容发现，且存在隐藏集成成本；3) 基于SNGP的不确定性驱动探索干预产生最大的新发布内容提升，但伴随可测量的参与度或多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 给出了关于应在哪一层进行干预的建议，以及每种选择的隐藏成本。

Abstract: Continuously trained ranking models in music recommenders fall into feedback loops where previously consumed items dominate recommendations. This suppresses two distinct content classes: new releases (temporal freshness) and unlistened catalog items (novelty). Industry practitioners have a wide menu of interventions available, ranging from serving-time heuristics, training-data reweighting, architectural debiasing, to uncertainty-driven exploration, each of which are well understood in academic settings. But live systems offer challenges with continuously ingested content, interconnected components, and practical limitations that counteract the findings from academic research.
  We report results from off-policy online A/B tests for six interventions and a combination experiment across four conceptual layers (serving, training, architecture, exploration) on the YouTube Music homepage. All interventions modify the ranking model or the serving layer that consumes its scores; candidate generation and other upstream components are held fixed. We discuss key takeaways from our results: first, serving-time interventions on continuously trained systems are neutralized by the learning loop. Second, architectural debiasing reduces popularity dominance and improves diversity but does not create discovery, while carrying hidden integration costs. Finally, uncertainty-driven exploration interventions with a Spectral-normalized Neural Gaussian Process (SNGP) head produce the largest new-release lift, though they come with a measurable engagement or diversity tradeoff. We close with recommendations on which layer to intervene at, and the hidden costs of each choice.

</details>


### [207] [Escaping the Euclidean Void: Manifold-Informed Flow Matching for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23762)
*Dengzhao Fang,Jingtong Gao,Yu Li,Xiangyu Zhao,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对连续生成式推荐中因欧几里得空间空洞导致离散物品语义缺失的问题，提出MIRAGE框架，利用物品共现图作为语义流形的代理来矫正嵌入几何结构，在不改变原始概率路径的前提下将插值状态对齐到局部锚点，实现高效的一步推理，在四个数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续生成式推荐（基于流匹配）在离散物品目录上会穿越缺乏有效语义证据的连续区域（称为"欧几里得空洞"），导致生成轨迹偏离有效物品语义支持。

Method: 提出MIRAGE框架，利用物品共现图作为底层语义流形的代理，将插值路径状态与局部锚点对齐，从而矫正嵌入几何结构；保留原始概率路径，仅在训练阶段使用共现图。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上优于最先进基线，尤其提升了稀疏观测目标的推荐性能，同时保持鲁棒的整体准确性。

Conclusion: 通过流形感知的嵌入几何矫正，有效解决了连续生成式推荐中的欧几里得空洞问题，实现了准确且高效的一步推理。

Abstract: Conventional recommenders capture users' preferences by optimizing observed user-item relations, whereas continuous generative recommendation additionally learns the trajectory of synthesizing a target item. Flow matching drives this process by gradually shaping initial noise into a definitive next-item representation through intermediate states in a continuous embedding space. However, item catalogs are discrete and sparsely supported, meaning even a straight Euclidean path can cross continuous regions that contain little evidence of valid item semantics. Formalizing this failure as the Euclidean void, we propose MIRAGE, a Manifold-Informed Rectification framework for Accelerated Generation of Embeddings in sequential recommendation, which rectifies the learned embedding geometry around an unchanged straight probability path. By leveraging an item co-occurrence graph as a proxy for the underlying semantic manifold, MIRAGE aligns interpolated path states with local anchors, reorganizing the embedding space to ground the trajectory in valid item support. MIRAGE retains the original probability path and uses the graph only during training, thereby enabling accurate and efficient one-step inference. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets reveal that MIRAGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, effectively boosting performance on sparsely observed targets while achieving robust overall accuracy. Our code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [208] [ClawRec: A Claw-Native Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23779)
*Chenghao Wu,Kesha Ou,Xiaolei Wang,Bowen Zheng,Bingqian Li,Enze Liu,Wayne Xin Zhao,Weitao Li,Long Zhang,Sheng Chen,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨平台、跨内容形式的爪式原生推荐系统新范式ClawRec，它维护证据关联、时间结构化的用户状态，按功能角色组织检索，按边际效用选择候选，生成非冗余的推荐列表，实验表明其在NDCG@20和Hit@20上分别提升0.1126和0.0854。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统局限于单一平台，只观察局部交互、从孤立候选空间排序，无法应对用户在多源信息间搜索、消费和比较的实际任务。跨平台个人智能体创造了围绕用户而非平台进行推荐的机会。

Method: ClawRec维护一个证据关联、时间结构化的用户状态（连接跨平台行为与跨源推荐），按功能源角色组织检索，根据边际效用选择候选，生成无冗余、与用户当前任务对齐的推荐列表。

Result: ClawRec在ClawRec-SimBench上取得NDCG@20为0.6134（+0.1126）、Hit@20为0.6944（+0.0854），同时提升了用户状态质量和时间对齐性。

Conclusion: 跨平台、跨内容形式的爪式原生推荐范式优于传统单平台推荐方法，能够更好地服务于用户在多信息源间的复杂任务。

Abstract: Recommender systems have become integral to navigating the modern digital ecosystem. Yet most deployed systems remain confined within single-platform boundaries, observing localized interaction traces and ranking items from isolated candidate spaces. This design is poorly suited to real-world tasks that unfold through searches, content consumption, and comparisons across multiple information sources. Claw-style personal agents, with persistent access to authorized cross-platform context, create an opportunity for recommendation to operate around the user rather than any single platform. In this paper, we introduce Claw-native recommender systems, a new paradigm that moves beyond platform-local ranking to produce unified, complementary recommendation slates spanning diverse sources and content forms. To instantiate this paradigm, we present ClawRec, the first recommender system designed to operate natively in this environment. ClawRec maintains an evidence-linked, temporally structured user state that connects cross-platform behaviors with cross-source recommendations. It organizes retrieval around functional source roles and selects candidates according to their marginal utility, producing non-redundant slates aligned with the user's active task. To enable rigorous evaluation, we introduce ClawRec-SimBench, a benchmark constructed from sequences of concrete life events and cross-platform behavior trajectories. Experiments show that ClawRec outperforms the strongest baselines, achieving an NDCG@20 of 0.6134 (+0.1126) and a Hit@20 of 0.6944 (+0.0854), while also improving user state quality and temporal alignment. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/ClawRec.

</details>


### [209] [Who Gets Named: Citation Type Predicts Individual Naming by Grounded Language Models, and a Roster Instrument Captures 0.5% of It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23893)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究通过2,400次API调用，考察AI模型在推荐个人专业人士（如房地产经纪人、汽车经销商）时的可见性。发现模型在25.8%的回复中会指名道姓，行业类别影响最大（房地产35.4% vs 保险9.1%），模型间差异达四倍（Grok 38.0% vs Gemini 9.3%），英文提示语比本地语言更易引出个人姓名（36.7% vs 15.6%），且基于LinkedIn名册的测量方法只能覆盖极小且不具代表性的样本（0.47%匹配率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往AI品牌可见性研究聚焦于企业层面（模型是否推荐某公司），本研究将问题下沉到个体专业人士层面（模型是否推荐某个人），填补了AI推荐系统中个人可见性测量方法的空白。

Method: 在2026年7月24日两小时内，向四个模型（GPT-5.6 Sol、Gemini 3.6 Flash、Perplexity Sonar Pro、Grok 4.5）发出2,400次有 grounded API 调用，包含120条买家意图提示语、各5次迭代、4个欧洲市场和5种查询语言。采用级联规则编码（不依赖名册、排除美国城市同名检测，精确率96.9%、召回率61.7%），并用层级聚类校正（ICC=0.258，有效n=407）。同时构建939人LinkedIn名册进行匹配验证。

Result: 模型在25.8%回复中命名个人；行业类别差异显著（房地产35.4% > 汽车经销32.9% > 保险9.1%，p=5.8e-8）；模型间差异四倍（Grok 38.0% > Perplexity > GPT-5.6 > Gemini 9.3%）；引用类型而非数量预测命名行为；英文提示语命名率是本地语言的两倍以上（36.7% vs 15.6%，OR=3.14）；LinkedIn名册仅匹配0.47%的姓名提及，覆盖范围极小且不具代表性。

Conclusion: AI模型在专业人士推荐中存在显著的可见性偏差，受行业类别、模型选择和查询语言影响。传统的基于名册的测量方法严重低估了AI实际推荐个人的行为，建议采用更全面的 grounded API 方法进行测量。

Abstract: Prior work on AI brand visibility measures the firm: does a model recommend a company, and does that track its reputation. This study asks the question one level down, in categories where the buyer picks a person. It issued 2,400 grounded API calls in one two-hour window on 24 July 2026: 120 buyer-intent prompts, four models (GPT-5.6 Sol, Gemini 3.6 Flash, Perplexity Sonar Pro, Grok 4.5), five iterations each, four European markets and five query languages. Every response was coded for whether it named an individual professional, by a rule cascade that never consults a roster and that drops detections resolving to a same-named American city (precision 96.9%, recall 61.7%, so every rate below is a lower bound). All inference corrects for clustering within prompt: intraclass correlation 0.258, effective n 407 against a nominal 2,400. Models named an individual in 25.8% of responses. Category dominates: real estate 35.4% and car dealerships 32.9% against insurance 9.1% (chi-square 159.3, p = 5.8e-8 after correction). Models differ four-fold, from Grok 38.0% to Gemini 9.3%. Citation type predicts naming and citation volume does not: naming responses cite the individual's own site 2.6 points more often (95% CI +1.4 to +3.9) and category portals 4.3 points more often, and cite firm-owned pages at the same rate (44.1% against 45.5%). On nine matched translation pairs, English prompts named an individual in 36.7% of responses against 15.6% for the same question in the local language (OR 3.14, clustered p = 0.074, so the direction is clear and the design cannot close it). A 939-person roster built from public LinkedIn search matched 128 of 27,293 name-shaped mentions (0.47%), 26 of the 939 people were ever named, and the roster-derived rates of 0.0% to 25.4% measure that overlap. Roster-based measurement of individual AI visibility sees a small and unrepresentative slice of what models do.

</details>


### [210] [MEMOIR: Temporal Behavioral Memory for Recommendation Across the Preference-Drift Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23986)
*Younggue Bae*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MEMOIR 通过将用户交互历史分割为时间窗口、利用LLM生成每个时期的语义行为记忆，并聚合当前状态、演化方向和预测未来来生成统一用户表示。在Amazon Reviews 2023的两个品类上，MEMOIR与最强基线UniSRec在NDCG@10上几乎持平（0.0643 vs 0.0641），以2-2分裂四个指标。消融实验显示各组件单独贡献不大（均在全模型2%以内）。按偏好漂移分数分层分析发现，MEMOIR在高漂移和低漂移极端用户群体上领先排序质量指标，而UniSRec在所有漂移层上领先HR指标，并在中等漂移层上领先排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐方法通常将用户行为视为静态序列，忽略了用户偏好的时间动态性和演化模式，难以捕捉用户长期行为中的语义变化和漂移趋势。

Method: 提出MEMOIR框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 时间窗口分割用户交互历史；(2) 利用LLM生成每个窗口的语义行为记忆；(3) 聚合当前状态、演化方向和预测未来为统一用户表示。采用演化保持对比损失（evolution-preserving contrastive loss）及其方向一致性项（directional-consistency term）进行训练。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews 2023的Electronics和Clothing_Shoes_and_Jewelry两个品类上，MEMOIR与UniSRec在NDCG@10上统计持平（0.0643 vs 0.0641），以2-2分裂四个指标（MEMOIR领先NDCG@10和MRR，UniSRec领先HR@10和HR@20）。消融实验显示各组件仅贡献约2%的NDCG@10变化。分层分析揭示MEMOIR在高/低漂移极端用户上领先排序质量，UniSRec在所有漂移层上领先HR指标并在中等漂移层上领先排序质量。

Conclusion: MEMOIR的最实质性和可复现发现并非接近的聚合指标或任何单一组件，而是偏好漂移分层模式：模型在不同漂移程度的用户群体上表现出现显著差异。这一发现的深层原因留作开放问题供未来研究。

Abstract: We propose MEMOIR, a framework that segments user interaction histories into temporal windows, generates semantic behavioral memory for each period using an LLM, and aggregates current state, evolution direction, and predicted future into a single user representation. On the Electronics and Clothing_Shoes_and_Jewelry categories of Amazon Reviews 2023, MEMOIR is statistically tied with UniSRec, the strongest baseline, on aggregate NDCG@10 (0.0643 vs. 0.0641), splitting the four reported metrics 2-2: MEMOIR leads NDCG@10 and MRR, UniSRec leads HR@10 and HR@20. An ablation study finds that no single architectural component - the evolution-preserving contrastive loss, its directional-consistency term, or temporal window segmentation itself - individually explains much of MEMOIR's approximately 18% relative gain over ID-based SASRec; all four ablations land within 2% of the full model on aggregate NDCG@10. Stratifying test performance by a composite preference-drift score instead reveals where the gain concentrates: MEMOIR leads on ranking-quality metrics (NDCG@10, MRR) specifically among users at the high- and low-drift extremes of the distribution, while UniSRec leads the volume-oriented HR@10/HR@20 metrics across all drift strata and edges out MEMOIR on ranking quality in the middle band. We report this drift-stratified pattern, rather than the near-tied aggregate numbers or any single ablated component, as MEMOIR's most substantive and reproducible finding, and surface why it holds as an open question for future work.

</details>


### [211] [Mosaic: A Fleet of User Embedding Specialists for Recommendation at Meta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24015)
*John Zhiyuan Zheng,Xian Sun,Xiangyang Mou,Yujunrong Ma,Christina You,Michael Jiayuan He,Hrishikesh Paranjape,Aakarsha Agarwal,Hong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Mosaic是一个工业级用户建模平台，采用多专家融合（4种异构模型家族）学习用户嵌入，通过MRM和CRL技术最大化专家间信息增益，并引入CoEval和User Tower Zero-Out实现免日志评估，混合CPU/GPU服务栈支持灵活部署，在离线NE和在线指标上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统中用户表示是高杠杆建模问题，现有方法要么使用单一模型输出少量嵌入向量，要么采用共享骨干网络加任务适配。作者希望构建一个能够融合多种异构模型家族、最大化各专家边际信息贡献、同时满足多样化服务需求的用户建模平台。

Method: 1. **专家舰队**：四种异构模型家族——记忆驱动型、密集重型、序列基模型和CoTrain模型，各聚焦用户行为的不同方面。2. **MRM（多任务关系挖掘）**：最大化新专家对现有专家组合的边际信息贡献。3. **CRL（余弦冗余损失）**：通过余弦相似度正则化减少专家间的表征冗余。4. **CoEval & User Tower Zero-Out**：免日志的嵌入评估框架，将用户塔输出置零来隔离评估用户嵌入质量。5. **混合CPU/GPU在线离线服务栈**：各专家可选择适合的推理策略以满足新鲜度、延迟和计算需求。

Result: 离线NE（归一化熵）指标一致且显著改善，同时在线指标也取得了正向收益。

Conclusion: Mosaic通过多专家异构融合与创新的信息增益最大化技术，在工业级推荐系统中验证了用户表示建模的规模化方案的有效性，为后续用户建模平台的设计提供了重要的工程与算法经验。

Abstract: User representation is one of the highest-leverage modeling problems in industrial recommendation systems: a single advancement in how users are encoded can propagate across retrieval, ranking, and integrity tasks at platform scale. Prior industrial user representation work builds either a single user model that emits one or more embedding vectors or a shared backbone with task-specific adaptation. In this paper, we present Mosaic, a foundational user modeling platform that employs a fleet of specialists to learn user embeddings. The fleet comprises four architecturally diverse model families - memorization-driven, dense-heavy, sequential-based, and CoTrain models - each focusing on a distinct facet of user behavior. We developed MRM (Multi-task Relations Mining) and CRL (Cosine Redundancy Loss) techniques to maximize the marginal information contribution of each new specialist. We also introduce CoEval and User Tower Zero-Out, new logging-free embedding evaluation framework that improves development velocity while preserving downstream-aligned accuracy. Our hybrid CPU/GPU, online-and-offline serving stack allows each specialist to choose the adequate serving strategy to meet the freshness, latency, and computational requirements. Mosaic delivers consistent and significant offline NE improvements in addition to online gains.

</details>


### [212] [SpecFormer: Mitigating Embedding and Attention Collapse via Spectral-Aware Transformer for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24025)
*Yu Cui,Yi Xu,Jiahao Wang,Hao Zhang,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Can Wang,Jinxin Hu,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer在推荐任务中性能不佳的根本原因——嵌入和注意力坍缩（embedding and attention collapse），并提出SpecFormer模型，通过可学习谱软化、谱软化注意力机制和谱残差位置编码解决该问题，在工业级和公开数据集上均取得显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的自注意力机制直接应用于推荐任务时性能往往不佳，甚至不如精心设计的简单推荐模型。作者揭示了这一性能瓶颈源于推荐场景特有的严重嵌入和注意力坍缩问题，其根源在于推荐数据的异质性和长尾分布导致严重的谱坍缩（spectral collapse），即仅有少数主奇异值主导。这一现象在模型的向前和反向传播中形成恶性循环，加速了嵌入和注意力坍缩，并限制了模型随深度增加的可扩展能力。

Method: SpecFormer包含三个核心模块：1) 可学习谱软化模块（Learnable Spectral Softening），动态平滑输入token嵌入的奇异值分布；2) 谱软化注意力机制（Spectrum-softened Attention），在更均匀的谱分布空间中进行特征交互建模；3) 谱残差位置编码（Spectral Residual Position Encoding），通过奇异值的泰勒展开显式为特征交互提供谱归纳偏置。

Result: 在1个工业级数据集和2个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，SpecFormer显著优于当前最先进的基线方法。该模型已在真实商业推荐系统中成功部署，并展现出卓越的可扩展能力：堆叠SpecFormer层能够有效提升注意力的有效秩（effective rank）和推荐性能。

Conclusion: SpecFormer通过引入谱感知机制有效缓解了推荐场景中Transformer的嵌入和注意力坍缩问题，打破了谱坍缩导致的恶性循环，实现了更好的模型可扩展性与推荐性能，验证了谱正则化在推荐Transformer中的重要性。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains; however, directly applying their standard self-attention mechanism to recommendation often yields suboptimal performance, sometimes even trailing behind well-designed simple recommendation models. In this paper, we reveal that this performance bottleneck stems from severe embedding and attention collapse unique to recommendation scenarios. The heterogeneity and long-tail nature of recommendation data lead to a severe spectral collapse dominated by a few principal singular values. We further theoretically demonstrate that this triggers a vicious cycle in recommendation model's forward and backward propagation, which accelerates embedding and attention collapse and limits the model's scaling capability with increased depth. To address these issues, we propose SpecFormer, a novel Spectral-Aware Transformer designed for mitigating embedding and attention collapse in recommendation. Specifically, SpecFormer introduces 1) a Learnable Spectral Softening module to dynamically smooth the singular values distribution of the input token embeddings; 2) a Spectrum-softened Attention mechanism to model feature interaction under a more uniform spectral distribution space; 3) a Spectral Residual Position Encoding via Taylor expansion of singular values, explicitly providing a spectral inductive bias for feature interactions. Extensive experiments on one industrial and two public datasets demonstrate that SpecFormer significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, SpecFormer has been successfully deployed in a real-world commercial recommender system and exhibits exceptional scaling capabilities: stacking SpecFormer layers actively improves the attention effective rank and recommendation performance.

</details>


### [213] [ConAlign: Conditional Alignment Framework for Balancing Biased and Unbiased Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24092)
*Jingcheng Zhang,Yihan Wang,Qi Song,Liyin Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ConAlign是一种面向工业部署的条件性去偏框架，通过离散门控的条件对齐机制，在保持事实准确性的同时无偏估计用户偏好，支持实时流式自适应，首个成功部署于大规模工业推荐系统的流式去偏框架，在快手大规模在线A/B测试中显著提升了长期用户参与度和兴趣多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统因训练数据存在各种偏差导致过滤气泡，用户兴趣退化到狭窄类别，严重损害长期参与度。现有基于无偏均匀数据的去偏方法因忽视事实推荐性能和高计算开销而无法工业部署。

Method: 提出ConAlign条件对齐框架，核心创新是离散门控的条件对齐机制，选择性地从有偏塔向无偏塔迁移知识，采用选择性干预而非全盘纠正范式，平衡事实准确性与无偏偏好估计，支持实时流式自适应。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的离线实验验证了有效性；在快手的大规模在线A/B测试中，显著提升了长期用户参与度和兴趣多样性，且延迟开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: ConAlign是首个成功部署于大规模工业推荐系统的流式去偏框架，利用少量无偏随机流量实现去偏，在事实准确性与无偏估计之间取得平衡，并支持实时流式更新。

Abstract: Industry recommender systems trained on observational data suffer from various biases that create filter bubbles, causing user interests to collapse into narrow categories and severely degrading long-term engagement. While utilizing unbiased uniform data for debiasing has shown promise, existing methods remain impractical for industrial deployment due to limitations such as neglect of factual (biased) recommendation performance and the substantial computational overhead. To overcome these limitations, we propose ConAlign (Conditional Alignment Framework), a conditional debiasing approach for industrial deployment. The key innovation of ConAlign lies in a discrete gating-based conditional alignment mechanism that selectively transfers knowledge from the biased tower to the unbiased tower. Following a selective intervention paradigm rather than universal correction, it seamlessly balances factual accuracy and unbiased preference estimation while supporting real-time streaming adaptation. To the best of our knowledge, ConAlign is the first streaming debiasing recommendation framework successfully deployed in a large-scale industrial recommendation system that utilizes a small fraction of unbiased random traffic for debiasing. Extensive offline experiments on three real-world datasets rigorously validate the effectiveness of our proposed framework. Furthermore, large-scale online A/B testing on Kuaishou demonstrates significant improvements in long-term user engagement and interest diversity, with negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [214] [Strategy-Aware Parameter-Efficient Adaptation for LLM-based Auto-Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24232)
*Songyue Cai,Lianyu Wang,Shan Gu,Ziru Xu,Jian Xu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SAGE提出了一种基于大语言模型的策略感知自动竞价框架，通过参数高效的多模态对齐方法（位置增强、文本对齐、约束门控LoRA），在仅微调不到10%参数的情况下实现卓越的竞价性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动竞价方法存在轨迹-文本交互浅层化以及需要昂贵的全参数微调的问题，阻碍了预训练知识在多样化约束下的高效利用。

Method: SAGE包含三个核心模块：(i)位置增强模块采用时序-语义位置嵌入捕获动态语义结构；(ii)文本对齐模块利用门控交叉注意力对齐轨迹与文本模态的嵌入空间；(iii)约束门控LoRA模块以约束作为路由信号，仅激活少量专家来适配冻结的LLM行为。

Result: 在大规模自动竞价基准上，SAGE在仅微调不到10%的可训练参数下持续取得优于全参数微调的性能，消融实验验证了各模块的关键贡献。

Conclusion: SAGE通过参数高效的多模态对齐框架有效解决了LLM在自动竞价中的高效适配问题，为约束条件下的自动竞价提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Advertising bidding has evolved from manual strategies to auto-bidding systems better adapted for large-scale, dynamic auction environments. While recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong reasoning for auto-bidding, existing methods suffer from shallow trajectory-text interactions and require costly fine-tuning, hindering the efficient use of pretrained knowledge under diverse constraints. To address these challenges, we propose SAGE, a novel Strategy-aware Auto-bidding framework Guided by LLMs for Efficient bidding. SAGE introduces a parameter-efficient multi-modal alignment framework for constrained auto-bidding with LLMs. Specifically, SAGE comprises three key components: (i) the position augmentation module adopts temporal-semantic positional embeddings to effectively capture the intrinsic dynamics and semantic structures; (ii) the text alignment module leverages gated cross-attention to align the embedding spaces of trajectory and text modalities, enabling effective multi-modal fusion while alleviating the computational overhead caused by long trajectories; (iii) the constraint-gated LoRA module employs constraints as routing signals, activating only a small subset of experts to adapt the behavior of a frozen LLM efficiently. Extensive experiments on large-scale auto-bidding benchmark demonstrate that SAGE consistently achieves superior performance while tuning less than 10% of the trainable parameters required by full fine-tuning. Ablation studies further validate the critical contribution of each component to the framework's overall performance.

</details>


### [215] [A Model-Driven Pipeline for Data Quality Specification and Operationalization: A No-Code Approach for Domain Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24245)
*Arno Kesper,Lukas Sebastian Hofmann,Markus Matoni,Gabriele Taentzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型驱动工程的流水线，通过QPM元模型和Constrainify web应用，将领域专家用自然语言表达的数据质量期望形式化为可执行的质量分析，解决了数据质量分析中领域专家与数据工程师之间的技术鸿沟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产等领域的数��数据由人工收集整理，容易出现不一致等质量问题。领域专家了解质量期望但缺乏将其形式化为可执行分析的技术能力，而数据工程师虽具备技术能力却不了解领域需求，这种依赖关系导致质量分析过程耗时且技术要求高，亟需一种降低技术门槛的方法。

Method: 提出一个形式化与操作化数据质量约束的流水线：使用QPM元模型定义可复用质量分析的模板；通过Constrainify web应用定制模板以适应特定概念需求；利用基于模型驱动工程的子流水线工具链将模板翻译为可执行的质量分析。

Result: 得到一组可复用、可重复且领域特定的质量分析。

Conclusion: 该流水线有效解决了领域专家与数据工程师之间的协作障碍，使得非技术背景的领域专家也能参与数据质量分析的构建，提高了数据质量分析的效率和可复用性。

Abstract: High-quality data is essential for reliable analysis, decision-making, and research across domains. This is especially relevant in areas such as cultural heritage, where data is collected and curated manually, making it prone to quality issues like inconsistencies. To improve data quality, the data must be analyzed regularly using systematic quality analyses. Quality analyses validate the conformance of data to domain-specific expectations. These expectations are best understood by domain experts, who can express them using natural language. However, they rarely possess the technical expertise to formalize these expectations into executable quality analyses. Consequently, this process requires domain experts and data engineers, making it time-consuming and technically demanding. The required technical expertise and the resulting dependencies pose a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we present a pipeline for formalizing and operationalizing data quality constraints. We support this pipeline using QPM, a metamodel for defining templates for reusable quality analyses. The web application Constrainify enables tailoring templates to specific conceptual requirements and translating them into executable quality analyses via a tool-chain based on model-driven engineering subpipelines. The result is a set of reusable, repeatable, and domain-specific quality analyses.

</details>


### [216] [OxygenREC-v2: Internalizing Discrimination into Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24255)
*Guo Tang,Hanye Wu,Changjiang Han,Qingyang Li,Ming Zhang,Xiangyu Qian,Yanchen Qiao,Huanjie Wang,Zhi Ma,Zhen Li,Yaqiang Zang,Pinghua Gong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OxygenREC-v2，一种将行为信号内化到生成式推荐中的方法，通过行为指令条件化生成和熵感知轨迹优化自蒸馏框架，无需额外判别目标或奖励模型，在京东电商平台上线后UCTCVR提升1.6-4.4%，GMV提升2.8-6.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐在融入点击、加购、下单等行为信号时面临挑战：联合优化生成与判别目标需要精细权衡，使用独立排序器作为RL奖励则存在分布外评分与奖励不对齐问题。因此需要一种在不引入额外判别目标或奖励模型的前提下，将行为信号有效融入生成式推荐的方法。

Method: 提出Internalizes Discrimination into Generative Recommendation (IDGR)框架。预训练阶段使用行为指令（behavior instruction）对生成过程进行条件化；后训练阶段将未来交互行为作为特权知识，在熵感知轨迹优化自蒸馏框架（entropy-aware trajectory optimization self-distillation）中进行无需奖励模型的策略优化。整个过程中保持单一统一骨干网络，模型结构为3B参数、1B激活的MoE。

Result: 在京东电商平台线上A/B测试中，OxygenREC-v2相比OxygenREC-v1，用户点击转化率（UCTCVR）提升1.6-4.4%，GMV提升2.8-6.8%。

Conclusion: 通过将行为信号内化到生成过程中而非外挂判别头或奖励模型，可以有效提升生成式推荐系统的性能，验证了IDGR范式的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation unifies retrieval and ranking within a single model by autoregressively decoding semantic identifier (SID) sequences. Yet reliably incorporating behavior signals from clicks, cart additions, and orders remains challenging. Existing approaches either jointly optimize generative and discriminative objectives, requiring delicate trade-offs, or use a separate ranker as a post-hoc reinforcement-learning reward, risking out-of-distribution scoring and reward misalignment. We propose OxygenREC-v2, a generative recommender that Internalizes Discrimination into Generative Recommendation (IDGR). Rather than adding a separate discriminative objective, OxygenREC-v2 uses logged behavior to condition generation and supervise training. During pre-training, a behavior instruction conditions generation on the target behavior. During post-training, future interaction behaviors are exploited as privileged knowledge in our entropy-aware trajectory optimization self-distillation framework, enabling reward-model-free policy optimization. Throughout both training stages, OxygenREC-v2 maintains a single unified backbone. We implement OxygenREC-v2 as a 3B-parameter, 1B-activated MoE and deploy it on JD.com's large-scale e-commerce platform. Across multiple online A/B tests, OxygenREC-v2 improves user click-through conversion rate (UCTCVR) by 1.6--4.4% and GMV by 2.8--6.8% over OxygenREC-v1.

</details>


### [217] [Energy Constrained Hierarchical Underwater Monitoring via Local Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24313)
*Mohamed Amine Janati,Laurent Gautier,Stéphane Barbot*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层级式主从架构的水下监测系统，结合超低功耗MAX78000/MAX78002微控制器进行持续边缘感知，与NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX按需激活进行本地多模态推理，利用BioCLIP/OpenCLIP嵌入和LangChain多智能体框架实现物种识别、知识检索与自动报告生成，在降低能耗的同时生成结构化研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下生命监测面临严格能量约束、水下连接性差以及从远程部署点传输原始多模态数据成本高昂的挑战，亟需一种低能耗且能本地化智能处理的解决方案。

Method: 采用层级主从架构：MAX78000/MAX78002超低功耗微控制器持续监控视觉和声学信号，NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX在计划处理、事件驱动分析或研究者交互时被激活。激活后运行全本地多模态流水线，包括数据摄取、视觉目标提取、基于嵌入的索引、物种识别、检索增强推理和自动报告。使用BioCLIP/OpenCLIP嵌入组织任务数据、海洋分类学参考文献、科学文档和操作元数据存入本地ChromaDB集合。识别层结合视觉相似性搜索、质心分类和监督分类器。LangChain多智能体框架协调查询路由、结构化分析、能量管理、硬件重配置和报告生成。

Result: 通过视觉和声学监测案例研究进行系统评估，实现了超低功耗连续感知与本地多模态智能的桥接，可生成结构化、研究者可直接使用的知识，同时压缩本地数据以灵活传输，最小化能耗和通信开销。

Conclusion: 该架构使水下监测站能够在极低能耗约束下执行复杂的多模态分析任务，输出结构化的研究成果，并通过数据压缩减少通信负担，为远程水下自主监测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Marine life monitoring is limited by strict energy constraints, poor underwater connectivity, and the high cost of transmitting raw multimodal data from remote deployments. This paper proposes a low-consumption underwater monitoring architecture that combines always-on edge sensing with selective high-performance local reasoning. The system follows a hierarchical master--satellite design in which ultra-low-power MAX78000/MAX78002 microcontrollers continuously monitor visual and acoustic signals, while an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX is activated only for scheduled processing, event-driven analysis, or researcher interaction. Once active, the Jetson executes a fully local multimodal pipeline for data ingestion, visual target extraction, embedding-based indexing, species identification, retrieval-augmented reasoning, and automated reporting. BioCLIP/OpenCLIP embeddings are used to organize mission data, marine taxonomic references, scientific documents, and operational metadata in local ChromaDB collections. A dedicated identification layer combines visual similarity search, centroid-based classification, and supervised classifiers to support adaptive species recognition. A LangChain-based multi-agent framework coordinates query routing, structured analysis, energy management, hardware reconfiguration, and report generation. The architecture is evaluated through visual and acoustic monitoring case studies. The proposed system bridges ultra-low-power continuous sensing with local multimodal intelligence, enabling underwater stations to produce structured, researcher-ready knowledge while compressing local data for flexible acoustic, optical, or satellite transmission, minimizing both energy use and communication overhead.

</details>


### [218] [CogRec: Structure-Cognitive Fast-and-Slow Reasoning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24402)
*Xiang Liu,Jingsong Su,Shuqi Zhao,Pengbo Mo,Yiming Qiu,Huimu Wang,Mingming Li,Jiao Dai,Jizhong Han,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CogRec提出了一种结构认知的快慢推理框架，通过SID路由（包含Match、LateralJump、Explore操作）在语义ID拓扑空间中进行显式推理，以增强生成式推荐。实验表明，结构化推理在精确匹配不足但可学习SID转换可用时最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义ID的生成式推荐方法主要将SID作为需要记忆的目标序列，未能充分利用SID中的层次结构、层内关系和物品邻域作为显式推理空间；自然语言推理与最终的SID预测空间耦合较弱。

Method: 提出CogRec框架：1）增强SID垂直层次结构（层内语义图和物品邻域）；2）引入SID路由，包括Match（精确匹配，快速语义定位）、LateralJump（横向跳转）和Explore（探索操作，实现慢速结构导航）；3）监督多阶段pipeline对齐SID token、建立直接SID生成，在共享检查点下训练自然语言和SID路由推理分支，使用trie约束的输出空间。

Result: 在三个公开序列推荐基准上，SID路由相比直接生成方法有提升；结构化推理在前缀匹配不足但可学习SID转换可用时最有效，而长路径或弱支持的路径会引入额外解码成本和累积误差。

Conclusion: 在SID拓扑空间中进行结构化的显式推理能够提升生成式推荐性能，但需平衡推理路径长度与准确性。

Abstract: Semantic-ID-based generative recommendation represents each item as a hierarchical discrete token sequence and reformulates next-item prediction as constrained sequence generation. Existing methods, however, mainly use Semantic IDs as target sequences to be memorized, leaving the hierarchy, intra-layer relations, and item neighborhoods underused as an explicit reasoning space. Explicit reasoning-enhanced generative methods often produce a natural-language rationale before the item identifier, but this rationale is only weakly coupled with the discrete SID space in which the final prediction is made. We propose CogRec, a structure-cognitive fast-and-slow reasoning framework that grounds intermediate reasoning in the same SID topology used for target generation. CogRec augments the vertical SID hierarchy with intra-layer semantic graphs and item-level neighborhoods, and introduces SID Routing to represent recommendation reasoning through layer-wise Match, LateralJump, and Explore operations. Exact matching implements fast semantic localization, whereas lateral and exploratory operations instantiate slower structural navigation. A supervised multi-stage pipeline aligns the newly introduced SID tokens, establishes direct SID generation, and trains natural-language and SID-routing reasoning branches from a shared checkpoint under the same trie-constrained output space. Experiments on three public sequential-recommendation benchmarks show that SID Routing improves its corresponding direct-generation, indicate that structure-grounded reasoning is most useful when prefix matching is insufficient but learnable SID-space transitions remain available, whereas long or weakly supported routes introduce additional decoding cost and accumulated errors. Code is available at https://github.com/caskcsg/CogRec

</details>


### [219] [CORE: A Unified Cascaded Ordinal Relevance Estimation Framework for E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24417)
*Zhi Jin,Xi Wang,Yunfei Li,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对电商搜索中相关性排序的序数分类特性被传统多分类框架忽视的问题，提出统一级联二分类框架，将相关性估计重构为从高到低的序贯决策过程，同时适配大语言模型推理和在线BERT模型。LLM侧设计逐步推理与剪枝策略，BERT侧替换分类头为逐级二分类器并通过知识蒸馏从LLM迁移能力。离线评估与在线A/B实验验证框架有效性，在线bad-case率降低15.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多分类框架将相关性排序视为普通多分类问题，忽略了相关性等级间的自然序数关系，对相邻误分和远距离误分施加同等惩罚，导致学习目标与相关性评估的实际需求不匹配。

Method: 提出统一级联二分类框架：1）LLM推理采用逐步推理流程，结合剪枝策略和层级奖励函数；2）在线BERT模型将传统分类头替换为多个逐级二分类器，并通过知识蒸馏从LLM获取能力。

Result: 离线工业基准评估和在线A/B实验表明，该框架显著提升了相关性性能，在线bad-case率降低15.94%。

Conclusion: 逐级建模对相关性估计是有效的，级联二分类框架能够更好地对齐相关性排序的序数本质。

Abstract: Ranking relevance is a fundamental task in e-commerce search, directly affecting ranking quality and consumer experience. Although inherently an ordinal classification problem, it is commonly formulated as conventional multi-class classification, which overlooks the natural order among relevance levels and assigns equal penalties to adjacent and distant misclassifications. This mismatch leads to suboptimal learning objectives for practical relevance evaluation. To address this issue, we propose a unified cascaded binary classification framework applicable to both large language model inference and online BERT-based inference, which reformulates relevance estimation as a sequential decision process and decomposes multi-class prediction into a series of ordered binary judgments from higher to lower relevance tiers. For large language models, we design a step-wise reasoning procedure with pruning strategies and tier-specific reward functions. For the online BERT model, we replace the conventional classification head with multiple level-wise binary classifiers and distill the capabilities of large language models into the online model. Extensive offline industrial benchmark evaluations and online A/B experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework substantially improves relevance performance, reducing the online bad-case rate by 15.94\%. Further analyses suggest that tier-wise modeling is effective for relevance estimation.

</details>


### [220] [Unifying Generative Recall and Multi-Objective Ranking in a Single Decoder-Only Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24439)
*Ruochen Yang,Shuang Wen,Pengbo Xu,Yusheng Huang,Jiangxia Cao,Shuang Yang,Zhaojie Liu,Jiawei Sheng,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出UniR²，一个统一的解码器Transformer，通过异构序列（用户上下文、SID轨迹、物品特征）和双查询前缀因果注意力机制，将生成式召回和多目标排序统一到单一模型中，在快手平台上离线实验和长期在线A/B测试均验证了其有效性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统中召回和排序两级级联架构存在目标不一致性、候选传递信息丢失以及用户端上下文重复计算的问题，而生成式召回和排序缩放共享相似的Transformer建模哲学，为统一集成创造了自然机会。

Method: 提出UniR²统一解码器Transformer，设计异构序列（用户上下文+SID轨迹+物品特征），其中生成轨迹作为召回与排序间的表示桥梁；采用双查询前缀因果注意力实现任务特定的信息可见性，两任务共享基础注意力权重但保留独立优化边界，排序侧使用LoRA保持排序适应性而不干扰生成骨干网络。

Result: 在工业大规模数据上的离线实验证明了UniR²在召回和排序上的有效性和效率；快手平台上的长期在线A/B测试进一步显示了一致的正向收益，验证了统一模型在大规模推荐系统中的实用性。

Conclusion: UniR²通过统一的解码器Transformer架构成功融合了生成式召回和多目标排序，解决了级联架构的关键问题，在大规模工业推荐系统中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommendation systems typically separate recall and ranking into two independent stages. Although this cascade supports corpus-level retrieval and fine-grained multi-objective scoring, it causes objective inconsistency, information loss at the candidate hand-off, and redundant user-side context computation. Meanwhile, the generative recall and ranking scaling share a common Transformer-based modeling philosophy, where architectural consistency creates a natural opportunity for unified integration. However, direct sharing remains challenging since the two tasks require different information visibility and optimization methods. Therefore, we propose \textbf{UniR$^2$}, a \textbf{Uni}fied decoder-only Transformer that unifies Generative \textbf{R}ecall and Multi-Objective \textbf{R}anking within a single heterogeneous sequence comprising user context, SID trajectory, and item features. Within this sequence, the generated trajectory serves as a representation bridge between recall and ranking, where Dual-Query Prefix-Causal Attention provides task-specific visibility. The two tasks share the base attention weights but retain separate optimization boundaries, with ranking-side LoRA preserving ranking adaptability without disrupting the generative backbone. Extensive offline experiments on large-scale industrial data demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of UniR$^2$ for both recall and ranking. Long-term online A/B tests on Kuaishou platform further show consistent positive gains, validating the practicality of unified model in large-scale recommendation systems.

</details>


### [221] [Evaluating RAG for French immigration law: a benchmark and baseline study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24449)
*Annia Abtout,Julien Delaunay,Monika Ewa Rakoczy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对法国国际招聘中复杂的法律框架，该研究构建了首个公开基准测试，涵盖许可证类型推荐、所需文件检索和法律引用覆盖。通过在52个标注合成档案上对比纯参数化LLM与稠密检索增强（Qwen3.5-9B和27B），发现检索增强在两个规模下均能提升行政指导的可靠性，尤其在许可证类型准确性方面最为显著，证实了检索基础对于该领域可靠行政指导的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI基准测试未能覆盖法国国际招聘中多层级法律框架的复杂性，缺乏针对该领域的公开评估标准。

Method: 构建公开基准测试，涵盖许可证类型推荐、所需文件检索和法律引用覆盖三个任务；在52个标注合成档案上，对比纯参数化LLM基线（Qwen3.5）与稠密检索增强方法在两种模型规模（9B和27B）上的表现。

Result: 检索增强在两个模型规模下均能改善行政指导质量，其中许可证类型推荐准确性的提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 检索基础对于法国国际招聘领域获得更可靠的行政指导至关重要，并值得进一步探索混合检索策略。

Abstract: International recruitment in France requires navigating a layered legal framework absent from existing legal AI benchmarks. We present a publicly available benchmark and first comparative evaluation for this domain, covering permit-type recommendation, required-document retrieval, and legal citation coverage. Comparing a parametric LLM baseline against dense retrieval augmentation at two model scales (Qwen3.5-9B and -27B) on 52 annotated synthetic profiles, we find that retrieval improves administrative guidance at both scales, most notably permit-type accuracy. Our results confirm that retrieval grounding is important for more reliable administrative guidance in this domain, and motivate further investigation of hybrid retrieval strategies.

</details>


### [222] [Robust Interpretation of Historical Documents in Knowledge Graphs Through Query Inference and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24475)
*Sebastià Nicolau,Adrià Molina,Oriol Ramos Terrades,Josep Lladós*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半符号化智能检索系统，结合词点检测（word-spotting）后OCR纠错与知识图谱表示，通过智能体化的GraphRAG架构实现对历史数据的准确、可验证访问，在保留大语言模型灵活性的同时，增强了应对OCR错误和幻觉的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数字图书馆和历史档案领域被广泛集成，但其可靠性和可信度令人担忧。如何在利用LLM泛化能力的同时，不损失档案机构所需的问责水平，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 采用半符号化框架，将词点检测（word-spotting）技术用于后OCR纠错，结合知识图谱表示使智能体能够通过合成查询访问信息。词点检测与代码生成交替协作，构建鲁棒的检索查询，在噪声和不确定性较高时仍可利用近似搜索。

Result: 在真实条件下（含OCR和转录错误）比较了传统RAG与智能体GraphRAG架构在历史信息检索上的表现，所提方法在应对误解释和幻觉方面更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 智能体化GraphRAG结合半符号化纠错与知识图谱查询，能在保持LLM灵活性的同时，显著提升历史档案检索的准确性和可验证性。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has redefined how users interact with information in digital environments. However, their widespread and often indiscriminate integration has raised significant concerns regarding reliability and trustworthiness issues that are particularly critical when accessing digital libraries and historical archives. How can one leverage the generalization capacity of an LLM without losing the level of accountability required for an archival institution? In this paper, we present an agentic retrieval system designed to deliver more accurate and verifiable access to historical data while preserving much of the flexibility associated with unconstrained LLMs. As a contribution to historical document analysis, we compare traditional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) with an agentic GraphRAG architecture in their ability to deliver historical information under realistic conditions, including the presence of OCR and transcription errors.
  We introduce a semi-symbolic framework that integrates word-spotting techniques for post-OCR correction with a knowledge graph representation that enables the agent to access information through synthesized queries. The interleaved collaboration between word spotting and code generation allows the agent to construct strong retrieval queries that are robust to misinterpretation and hallucination, while still leveraging approximate search when noise and uncertainty, common in historical document analysis, would otherwise hinder precise retrieval.

</details>


### [223] [DeCoRAG: Cognitive Decoupling and Semantic-Aware Cropping for Complex Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24554)
*Shuo Wang,Kai Zhang,Wenyuan Huang,Yizheng Yu,Xia Liao,Junming Su,Qing Wang,Fang Xi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeCoRAG，一种基于"认知解耦"的多模态图RAG管道，通过语义锚点缓解VLM在处理稀疏语义高密度布局时的"视觉注意力下沉"问题，并借助区域感知剪裁（RAP-Crop）机制降低40.8%的提示词token开销，在复杂文档基准上语义通过率提升12.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG管道依赖VLM从高密度稀疏语义布局中解析信息，但VLM在局部化视觉证据、解释语义和提取关系时会出现"视觉注意力下沉"（Visual Attention Sink），导致严重的语义丢失；同时整页处理又带来巨大的计算开销。作者旨在纠正这一有缺陷的范式。

Method: 提出DeCoRAG管道，其图构建阶段先建立宏观语义锚点（Semantic Anchor）来中和注意力下沉，再通过区域感知剪裁（RAP-Crop）机制将推理空间从稠密噪声背景转向纯净的意图驱动语义簇，最终构建的图支持混合检索和答案生成。

Result: 在复杂文档基准上，DeCoRAG的语义通过率比最强基线提升12.5个百分点；在DocVQA上具备泛化能力。RAP-Crop将离线图构建的提示词token数量减少40.8%，且不牺牲端到端准确性。

Conclusion: VLM的"视觉注意力下沉"是边界驱动而非内容特定的失败机制，语义锚定可有效缓解该问题。DeCoRAG通过"认知解耦"范式从根本上纠正了传统耦合式视觉语义推理的缺陷，在精度和效率上均取得显著改进。

Abstract: Advancing multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for complex document understanding presents a formidable dual dilemma of accuracy and efficiency, particularly in graph RAG. Processing structurally sparse yet visually dense layouts, such as extracting a tiny data marker from a financial chart, often incurs computationally prohibitive token overhead while still triggering catastrophic hallucination. However, multimodal Graph RAG pipelines rely on graph-construction stages that assume Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can resolve sparse semantics within high-density layouts. We challenge this assumption, revealing that forcing VLMs to localize visual evidence, interpret semantics, and extract relations triggers a "Visual Attention Sink," a mechanism driving catastrophic semantic loss, while full-page processing incurs massive computational overhead. Controlled interventions verify that this failure is boundary-driven rather than content-specific and that semantic anchoring mitigates it. To fundamentally correct this flawed paradigm, we introduce DeCoRAG, a multimodal Graph RAG pipeline that shifts knowledge processing from coupled visual-semantic reasoning to "Cognitive Decoupling." Rather than passively processing raw pixels, its graph-construction stage establishes a macroscopic Semantic Anchor to neutralize the attention sink. This anchor subsequently drives our Region-Aware Pruning and Cropping (RAP-Crop) mechanism, shifting the reasoning space from dense, noisy backgrounds to purified, intent-driven semantic clusters. The resulting graph supports hybrid retrieval and answer generation. Across complex document benchmarks, DeCoRAG improves the semantic pass rate by up to 12.5 percentage points over the strongest baseline and generalizes to DocVQA. RAP-Crop reduces offline graph-construction prompt tokens by 40.8% without sacrificing end-to-end accuracy.

</details>


### [224] [One Graph, Multiple Gains: Single High-Quality Item-Item Graph for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24607)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Zewei Liu,Shujie Li,Yipeng Du,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IIMRec通过构建高质量物品-物品图，并在表示增强、交互图增强和优化增强三个阶段系统性地复用该图，显著提升了多模态推荐性能，在冷启动和稀疏交互场景下表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物品-物品图的多模态推荐方法通常使用噪点相似度边构建图，且仅将其用于单一的商品表示传播功能，未能充分利用图的潜力。

Method: (1) 预处理阶段融合语义与共现信号，通过邻域一致性边重加权（NCER）基于三元闭包原理构建高质量物品-物品图；(2) 表示增强：残差II门控（RIG）自适应控制物品吸收语义邻域信号；(3) 交互图增强：内容引导的UI图扩展，通过高置信度语义邻居引入虚拟用户-物品边；(4) 优化增强：II-邻域BPR增强（INA）将正物品的top邻居视为折扣软正样本。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，IIMRec一致优于现有基线方法，同时运行更快、GPU内存消耗更少，在冷启动和稀疏交互条件下增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过跨阶段复用高质量物品-物品图，能够有效提升多模态推荐的性能与效率，理论分析验证了NCER降低谱噪信比、RIG收敛到非退化门控机制、INA提供更紧的泛化界。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation leverages item multimodal features alongside collaborative signals to capture user preferences. While item-item graphs have become a key building block in advanced models, existing methods typically construct them with noisy similarity edges and limit their role to a single function of item-item representation propagation, leaving substantial potential untapped.
  In this paper, we propose IIMRec, a framework that constructs a single high-quality item-item graph during preprocessing and systematically reuses it across three stages of the recommendation pipeline: representation enhancement, interaction graph enhancement, and optimization enhancement. The graph is built by fusing semantic and co-occurrence signals, then refined via Neighborhood Consistency Edge Reweighting (NCER), which applies the triadic closure principle to amplify structurally reliable edges and suppress spurious ones. Once constructed, the graph is leveraged in three complementary ways: (1) Item-item propagation with a Residual II Gate (RIG) that adaptively controls per-item absorption of semantic neighborhood signals for representation enhancement; (2) A content-guided UI graph expansion that introduces virtual user-item edges through high-confidence semantic neighbors for interaction graph enhancement; (3) II-Neighbor BPR Augmentation (INA) that treats top neighbors of positive items as discounted soft positives for optimization enhancement.
  We provide theoretical analysis showing that NCER reduces the spectral noise-to-signal ratio, RIG converges to a non-degenerate gating regime, and INA yields a tighter generalization bound. Extensive experiments on four datasets demonstrate that IIMRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while running faster and consuming less GPU memory, with particularly strong gains under cold-start and sparse-interaction conditions.

</details>


### [225] [LaRec: Unleashing LLM-based Latent Reasoning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24617)
*Yu Xia,Zihan Lin,Wei Yang,Rui Zhong,Cheng Chen,Huan Ren,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LaRec是一个高效的生成式推荐框架，通过潜空间预训练（步骤级对齐+过程方向对齐）解决细粒度监督缺失问题，并通过个性化高斯混合分布采样实现多样化推理路径以探索用户多维兴趣，在多个数据集上优于现有基线且保持高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐方法依赖显式思维链（CoT），导致推理文本冗长且响应效率低下；潜推理虽能提升效率，但面临两个关键挑战：(1) 潜推理仅依赖最终标签的稀疏监督信号，难以有效指导多个隐式推理步骤的优化；(2) 确定性推理路径限制了用户多样化兴趣和偏好的探索，削弱了LLM的推荐能力。

Method: LaRec包含两个核心阶段：(1) 潜空间预训练（Latent Pre-training）：通过步骤级对齐（step-level alignment）和过程方向对齐（process direction alignment）为潜空间推理提供丰富的监督信号，赋予LLM潜推理能力。(2) 个性化RL微调（Personalized RL-tuning）：基于用户历史兴趣构建个性化高斯混合分布，在训练中随机采样不同的推理起始点，引导LLM在潜空间中遍历多样化的推理路径，高效探索用户多面兴趣。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，LaRec显著优于现有基线方法，且保持了可比的推理效率。

Conclusion: LaRec通过潜空间中的细粒度监督和多样化推理路径，有效平衡了LLM推荐系统的效率与效果，为解决潜推理在推荐任务中的监督不足和路径单一问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great promise in recommendation due to superior reasoning abilities. However, existing methods mainly rely on explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT), resulting in verbose reasoning texts and inefficient response times. latent reasoning aims to balance efficiency by thinking within a continuous latent space, yet it faces two major challenges: (1) Lack of Fine-grained Supervision: Latent reasoning relies solely on feedback from the final labels, providing sparse supervisory signals that struggle to effectively guide the optimization of multiple hidden reasoning steps. (2) Single Reasoning Path: The deterministic nature of latent reasoning impedes the exploration of users' diverse interests and preferences, thereby limiting the recommendation capabilities of LLMs. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{$LaRec$}, an efficient generative recommendation framework designed to unleash the potential of latent reasoning in LLMs. $LaRec$ consists of two core stages: First, we design Latent Pre-training that empowers LLMs with latent reasoning capabilities by providing rich supervisory signals to the latent space reasoning via step-level alignment and process direction alignment. Second, we introduce Personalized RL-tuning. Specifically, we construct a personalized Gaussian Mixture Distribution for each user based on their historical interests. By randomly sampling distinct reasoning starting points from this distribution during training, we guide the LLMs to traverse diverse reasoning paths within the latent space, enabling efficient exploration of user's multi-faceted interests. Experiments on multiple datasets show that $LaRec$ significantly outperforms existing baselines with comparable efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出C-VCE框架，通过概念瓶颈层将分类器直接内置到扩散模型中，无需依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器即可生成视觉反事实解释，在保持图像质量的同时实现可控的概念编辑和预测翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的反事实解释方法依赖外部分类器，但分类器在噪声图像上可靠性差，导致系统脆弱且难以部署。亟需一种不依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器的反事实生成方法。

Method: 在扩散模型中引入概念瓶颈层，将分类器内置于生成模型；用户可在采样过程中开关语义概念；添加概率正则化项平衡预测变化与图像保持；使用梯度掩码限制修改区域。

Result: 在CelebA等基准上，C-VCE匹配或提升了翻转率，生成的图像在视觉上更接近输入、失真更少，优于依赖外部噪声分类器的基线方法。

Conclusion: 暴露并控制内部概念层是使强大生成模型更易于理解和安全使用的有效途径，C-VCE为需要具体"如果-会怎样"图像的视觉系统提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [227] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SeT-Diff，首个面向高性能计算节点遥测数据的基础生成模型，利用扩散模型结合传感器语义描述，在重构、插补、预测和虚拟感知任务上表现优异，MAE低至0.033-0.047，并具备零样本排列稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC遥测机器学习方法依赖静态的固定位置传感器变量子集，仅适用于单一任务，当目标任务变化或传感器指标变动时模型失效，缺乏灵活性和通用性。

Method: 提出SeT-Diff扩散模型，将生成过程条件化于每个传感器的语义描述上，从而将系统动态与数据集结构解耦，实现跨任务和变传感器配置的泛化能力。

Result: 在真实超算数据集上，重构任务MAE为0.0470；零样本排列稳定性优异，传感器打乱后精度几乎无损；单一预训练模型支持数据插补、预测和虚拟感知，热推断MAE达0.033。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff可作为HPC系统的高效数据驱动数字孪生，具备灵活、通用和鲁棒的特性，解决了传统方法在任务变更和传感器变化时的失效问题。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [228] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QFoldAgent，一个用于五残基四面体晶格折叠的闭环多智能体框架。通过设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚项、VQE量子-经典流水线优化哈密顿量、反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代调整惩罚系数。在QDockBank基准上，中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å；在未见序列上，结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，87%的无效案例得以恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测严重依赖哈密顿量惩罚权重，但现有晶格工作流通常手动固定这些系数，且模拟仅评估极短片段，缺乏系统性的优化方法。

Method: 提出了一个闭环多智能体框架QFoldAgent，包含三个核心组件：1）设计智能体（Design Agent）提出序列条件惩罚项；2）基于VQE的量子-经典流水线在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量；3）反馈智能体（Feedback Agent）利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代调整惩罚系数。整个过程中RMSD等真实指标对智能体不可见，仅用于评估。

Result: 在QDockBank基准上，中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å，最难目标上提升最大。在未见序列上，闭环将结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，恢复87%的初始无效案例；最强控制器在87%的序列上提升了第3轮能量，同时保持96%的拉氏图允许几何。

Conclusion: 迭代智能体控制能够系统地改善五残基量子环境中的优化行为并减少失败案例。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [229] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在标准基准测试中表现良好，但在不同措辞但语义等价的变体下，模型输出经常波动。研究发现实例级别的答案不稳定，翻转率超过23%，单次提示的正确性不能代表可靠性。但模型通常至少在一个变体上给出正确答案，表明知识存在但检索不稳定，可通过自释义策略部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试仅评估单次提示下的准确率，无法反映LLM在等价语义变体下的可靠性。作者旨在揭示这种隐蔽的不稳定性，并提出更可靠的评估方法。

Method: 在四个基准（事实问答和数学推理）和13个模型上，使用保义改写生成语义等价的多种提示，比较模型在原始版本与改写版本之间的答案一致性，分析实例级别的答案翻转率。

Result: 整体准确率变化不大，但实例级行为极不稳定。许多问题在改写后答案翻转，翻转率超过23%。在原始回答正确的样本上，改写的失败率更高。但模型通常至少在一个变体上能给出正确答案。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标掩盖了极大的不稳定性，评估等价输入下的一致性才能更真实地反映LLM的可靠性。自释义策略可在推理时部分恢复模型潜在的、但未一致检索到的知识。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [230] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepLens Diagnosis Agent，一个五阶段诊断管道，以小型医疗推理模型（7B参数）为核心，结合RAG和严格流程约束，在DiagnosisArena基准上达到60.14%的top-1诊断准确率，比无管道版本提升36个百分点，且成本低于Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro，同时性能超越它们。管道产生的结构化中间产物支持可审计和可溯源的错误定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM虽然是强大的通才，但单次提示（single-shot prompting）在医疗诊断中往往产生脆弱的推理结果。医疗诊断本质上是多阶段过程（事实提取→知识查询→鉴别分析→最优诊断选择），需要更结构化的方法。

Method: 提出五阶段管道：结构化临床提取（structured clinical extraction）、纪律性检索（disciplined retrieval）、约束候选生成（constrained candidate generation）、显式证据三角验证（explicit evidence triangulation）和可审计最终决策（auditable final decision）。核心模型为JSL Medical Small 7B v2，结合RAG。

Result: 在915例DiagnosisArena基准上top-1准确率60.14%，为小型和中型模型中最高；无管道版本仅23.99%（+36pp提升）；成本$0.0072/例（24K tokens），延迟24秒，比Claude Sonnet 4.5（$0.0110）和Gemini 3.1 Pro（$0.0128）便宜35-45%且性能分别高出9.70pp和9.17pp。

Conclusion: 工作流约束（harnessing）可以胜过参数量或API成本带来的优势；不确定性下的诊断推理需要的不仅是知识回忆（knowledge recall）；结构化中间产物支持高 stakes 场景下的可追溯性、可复现性和可审计性。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [231] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIITA提出了一种记忆驱动的推理时自适应框架，通过存储监督经验的修正方向原型和语义锚点，在推理时进行门控临时隐藏状态适配，在不更新主干网络、不扩展提示或不进行测试时反向传播的情况下，实现对小型语言模型的持续学习，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（SLM）在资源受限场景下进行持续学习时，直接更新参数空间会导致灾难性遗忘。现有针对大语言模型（LLM）的记忆方法依赖大量存储和强上下文推理能力，SLM不具备这些条件。因此需要一种适合SLM的资源高效持续学习方法。

Method: MIITA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将监督经验存储为紧凑的修正方向原型与语义锚点；(2) 推理时基于语义和不确定性线索检索相关方向；(3) 通过门控临时隐藏状态适配应用检索到的方向，无需更新主干网络、扩展提示或测试时反向传播。

Result: 在多种监督持续学习设置下的广泛实验表明，MIITA在固定记忆预算下持续提升了最终性能并有效缓解了遗忘问题。

Conclusion: MIITA通过记忆驱动的推理时适配，为资源受限场景下的小型语言模型提供了一种有效的持续学习方案，其设计还有局部理论分析支持，与一阶损失减少、不确定性引导检索和方向覆盖等原理相关联。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues. The retrieved directions are applied through gated temporary hidden-state adaptation, enabling non-destructive reuse of past supervision without backbone updates, prompt extensions, or test-time backpropagation. A local theoretical analysis links this design to first-order loss reduction, uncertainty-guided retrieval, and directional coverage for retaining old-stage knowledge. Extensive experiments across diverse supervised CL settings show that MIITA consistently improves final performance and mitigates forgetting under fixed memory budgets.

</details>


### [232] [Codifying the Judge: Scalable Evaluation via Program Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22561)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Shengqi Qiu,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序蒸馏（Program Distillation）方法，通过将LLM评估逻辑蒸馏为可解释、低成本的程序委员会（PAJAMA系统），在多项基准上匹敌13B量级LLM评估性能，并在RewardBench以两个数量级更低的API成本实现更优奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge评估范式存在高成本、高延迟和决策不透明等局限性，严重制约其可扩展性和可靠性。为此，需要一种高效、透明且廉价的替代方案。

Method: 提出程序蒸馏（Program Distillation），将LLM的评估逻辑蒸馏为一组可直接打分程序（programmatic judges）。进一步构建PAJAMA系统：合成多个评估程序、聚合其决策形成联合裁决，并引入回退机制将低置信度案例选择性升级至LLM处理。

Result: 在5个数据集和4个模型家族上，程序化评估器可匹敌13B参数量LLM评估性能。PAJAMA通过程序输出作为路由信号，同时提升准确率和吞吐量，推进Pareto前沿。在RewardBench上，基于程序裁决蒸馏的奖励模型以两个数量级更低的API成本超越使用专有LLM标签训练的模型。

Conclusion: 程序蒸馏是一种简单高效的LLM评估替代方案，兼具透明性、可编辑性和低成本优势，在评估和奖励信号生成上均有出色表现，可有效替代或辅助LLM-as-a-judge范式。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [233] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SF-AMS是一种通过效用驱动的记忆生存机制来维护紧凑高价值记忆的框架，用动态重要性建模替代静态检索和启发式衰减，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-s数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在处理长上下文依赖时，冗余和无关信息会退化多步推理能力，现有静态检索和启发式衰减方法无法有效维护高质量记忆。

Method: 提出SF-AMS框架，包含：(1) 效用驱动的生存机制，从使用冗余度和时间信号更新记忆重要性；(2) 分层记忆结构，优先保留稳定的实体一致信息；(3) 复合重要性评分，融合语义和实体层级信号提升检索鲁棒性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上多跳推理提升+9.65 F1，GPT-4o-mini上时序推理提升+6.91 F1，开放域任务提升+6.53 F1，跨骨干网络表现优异。

Conclusion: 将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号是实现可靠长上下文推理的关键。

Abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem. The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [234] [Synthetic Scenario Generation for Evaluation of Industry 4.0 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22563)
*Sagar Chethan Kumar,Rohith Kanathur,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent，一个面向工业智能体的合成场景生成管道，通过自动生成符合IEC标准的智能电网变压器故障诊断场景，在保证场景质量的前提下将生成效率提升8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业智能体基准测试（如AssetOpsBench）依赖人工编写场景，覆盖的资产类别有限，缺乏可扩展的场景生成方法。

Method: 提出了ScenarioGeneratorAgent管道：构建证据驱动的资产画像，进行覆盖感知的场景预算分配，通过混合验证-修复循环确保模式有效性、工具可达性、物理合理性、标准一致性和去重。采用两级缓存、并行焦点组生成、线程池卸载、批量LLM调用和早期拒绝过滤等优化策略。

Result: 在智能电网变压器场景生成中，50个场景的端到端运行时间降低8倍，复合质量评分74.2±1.9，与未优化基线73.8±3.0相比质量无损。

Conclusion: 基于标准约束的合成场景生成能够高效扩展工业智能体基准测试，同时保持场景质量。

Abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation.
